# TRG Prime Core — Automation Audit (3.697.0)

17 November 2026. Generated by `tools/audit-inventory.mjs` and `gen_audit.py` from the 3.697.0 packs and source. Rules reference: TRG Prime Core 3.8 (supplied by Graham 17 Nov 2026).

## How to read this

- **Every mechanically relevant core compendium entry is listed** below: 1,992 entries in 28 packs (macros, vehicles and test items are excluded).
- **"Routes"** are the machinery the measurement found: `data` (effect changes or engine flags), `structured` (the entry's own rule fields), `engine` (read by type, e.g. a weapon), `grants`, `code` (named in the rules source with comments stripped), `composed` (a feat name a table builds), `parsed` (a condition read off the text at use time).
- **"Check"** means a `verify-*.mjs` file names the entry; **"live"** means a live-testing report in the repository names it.
- **Measuring that machinery exists does not prove it is right.** The A–D class is a judgement. For the 1,883 entries with a route, the judgement was made rule by rule in the forty-block Core audit (claude/audit-pages-*.md, Sep–Oct 2026) and is not repeated here. For the 109 entries with no route at all (and the 9 repaired this session), the judgement was made in this session and is given in full.
- **Cross-checks against Core 3.8 done this session:** all 691 printed spell stat blocks (PP, tradition, cast AP, range, duration, traits): no discrepancy beyond class-name and "Penetrating/Penetrate" spellings. All 8 class tables (160 rows; BAB, BDB, saves, bonus feats, caster level, PP, BSB): identical. Feat prerequisites: no discrepancy. Live: all 8 classes levelled 1→20→1 in the Test world match their tables at every level.
